type 4 · 22 components · 725 chars

  TD | ## #muse-chat
  TD | -# In Muse Lounge · 1,240 members. A channel can change when it answers, add instructions and switch extras; everything else follows Muse Lounge.
  SEP
  TD | **When it answers in #muse-chat**
  ROW
    SELECT mdset:2:set:c<G1>.<MUSECHAT>:r “When it answers…” 1-1
      * Same as Muse Lounge: Chatty — Follows Muse Lounge and changes when it does  (=)
      - Chatty — Answers @mentions and replies, plus that person's follow-ups for 2 minutes  (conversational)
      - Only when @mentioned — Answers only @mentions and replies to its messages  (when-addressed)
      - Off — Never answers in #muse-chat  (off)
  SEC | **Instructions for the Muse**
  SEC | Muse Lounge's instructions apply here. Added for #muse-chat: nothing
      [Edit](primary) mdset:2:mod:c<G1>.<MUSECHAT>:i:b
  SEC | **Image and video generation**
  SEC | Off · same as Muse Lounge
      [Turn on](success) mdset:2:val:c<G1>.<MUSECHAT>:~media.enabled:on:b
ROW
  [Pause #muse-chat](danger) mdset:2:pz:c<G1>.<MUSECHAT>
  [Why no reply?] mdset:2:pg:c<G1>.<MUSECHAT>:y
ROW

ROW

  TD | ## Muse Lounge
  TD | -# 1,240 members · Custom settings
  ROW
    SELECT mdset:2:pre:s<G1> “Apply a preset…” 1-1
      - Friends — Chatty, no caps, may use what it remembers about you  (friends)
      - Community — Only when @mentioned, public facts only, 20 replies per person a day  (community)
      - Work — Only when @mentioned, nothing personal, ignores other bots  (work)
  SEP

  ROW
    SELECT mdset:2:set:s<G1>:r “When it answers…” 1-1
      - Same as Defaults: Chatty — Follows Defaults and changes when it does  (=)
      * Chatty — Answers @mentions and replies, plus that person's follow-ups for 2 minutes  (conversational)
      - Only when @mentioned — What config.yaml says  (when-addressed)
      - Off — Quiet in this server, except channels set otherwise  (off)
  TD | **What it may say about you**
  ROW
    SELECT mdset:2:set:s<G1>:m “What it may say about you…” 1-1
      * Same as Defaults: Public facts only — Follows Defaults and changes when it does  (=)
      - Anything it remembers — Your job, location and other saved memory; risky in big servers  (everything)
      - Public facts only — Name, interests, public projects and opinions  (public-only)
      - Nothing personal — Replies as if it had no memory of you  (nothing)
  SEC | **Instructions for the Muse** · ● set in config.yaml
  SEC | “Friendly community. Playful is fine.” · Reply length: Normal
      [Edit](primary) mdset:2:mod:s<G1>:i:b
  SEC | **Replies per person per day**
  SEC | No cap here
      [Change] mdset:2:mod:s<G1>:c:b
  TD | **Extras**
  ROW
    [Welcome: Off · Turn on] mdset:2:val:s<G1>:~welcome.enabled:on:b
    [Image and video: Off · Turn on] mdset:2:val:s<G1>:~media.enabled:on:b
    [Extras settings] mdset:2:pg:s<G1>:e

  TD | ## DMs
  TD | -# Direct messages with the bot. Your own DMs always get answers unless Who can DM it is Nobody.
  SEP
  TD | **Who can DM it**
  ROW
    SELECT mdset:2:set:d:df “Who can DM it…” 1-1
      * Only you — Built-in default · Only the bot's owners  (owners)
      - You and people you pick — Owners, plus the people below  (list)
      - Anyone in your servers — Anyone who shares a server with the bot  (shared-servers)
      - Anyone — Anyone on Discord, strangers included  (anyone)
      - Nobody — No DM gets an answer, not even yours  (nobody)
  TD | **People you picked:** nobody
  TD | -# Picking someone switches Who can DM it to You and people you pick.
